# Power map - who is tied to whom (as of 2026-11-20)

Receipts only. Each edge = one documented tie (family / marriage / funding / employment / appointment / board / business deal / contract), with date and sources. No inference of intent or causation; where two facts sit in sequence (e.g., settlement then approval), sources are cited for each and no causal link is claimed.

Edges: 451 filed links + 15 Epstein-network edges (V = verified by primary/reputable source; I = partly supported). Register rows: `theatre=powermap` in register/claims.jsonl. Machine-readable: `powermap.json`; images: `powermap.png` + `powermap-<cluster>.png`.

## Palmer Luckey
- **Matt Gaetz ↔ Palmer Luckey** — *marriage/family*, 2021-08-21 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-08 (v1) · last checked 2026-10-08  
  MARRIAGE: Matt Gaetz married Ginger Luckey, sister of Oculus/Anduril founder Palmer Luckey, on Catalina Island on 2021-08-21 (engaged Dec 2020 at Mar-a-Lago). Palmer attended. This makes Palmer Luckey Gaetz's brother-in-law.  
  Sources: <https://apnews.com/article/matt-gaetz-ginger-luckey-married-b71f9465e7c85e80e879802964adac69> ; <https://www.independent.co.uk/news/world/americas/matt-gaetz-marries-ginger-luckey-b1906942.html>
- **Palmer Luckey ↔ Donald Trump** — *funding (fundraiser host)*, 2020-10 ; 2024-06 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-08 (v1) · last checked 2026-10-08  
  FUNDING: Palmer Luckey hosted a Trump fundraiser at his Southern California home in Oct 2020 (raised ~$10M per LA Times) and co-hosted a June 2024 Trump fundraiser in Newport Beach.  
  Sources: <https://www.cnbc.com/2020/10/16/palmer-luckey-to-host-trump-fundraiser-in-southern-california.html> ; <https://www.latimes.com/socal/daily-pilot/news/story/2020-10-19/private-trump-fundraiser-in-newport-beach-raises-10-million-for-reelection-campaign> ; <https://www.ocregister.com/2024/06/08/trump-supporters-line-the-streets-in-newport-beach-in-anticipation-of-his-visit/>
- **US Army ↔ Anduril** — *government contract*, 2026-03 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-08 (v1) · last checked 2026-10-08  
  CONTRACT: US Army awarded Anduril (founded by Palmer Luckey) a 10-year enterprise contract with a ceiling of up to $20B in March 2026 (ceiling, not obligated); first task order $87M counter-drone C2 on Lattice.  
  Sources: <https://defensescoop.com/2026/03/14/anduril-20-billion-dollar-army-contract/> ; <https://breakingdefense.com/2026/03/army-awards-anduril-counter-drone-task-order-as-first-in-new-20b-contract-vehicle/>
- **US Army ↔ Anduril** — *government contract*, 2025-09 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-08 (v1) · last checked 2026-10-08  
  CONTRACT: Army awarded Anduril (teamed with Meta) ~$159M OTA for Soldier Borne Mission Command (ex-IVAS) prototypes, Sept 2025 - reuniting Luckey with Meta, the firm that pushed him out in 2017.  
  Sources: <https://defensescoop.com/2025/09/08/army-sbmc-contract-awards-anduril-rivet-soldier-borne-mission-command/> ; <https://breakingdefense.com/2025/09/ivas-anduril-meta-rivet-virtual-reality-goggle-army-sbmc/>
- **US Navy ↔ Anduril** — *government contract*, 2026-10-06 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-09 (v3) · last checked 2026-10-09  
  CONTRACT: Navy awarded Anduril an up-to-$2.9B contract for Virginia-class submarine components (2026-10-06); Anduril is putting $3.7B into an Arsenal shipyard at Sparrows Point, MD. Trump called Luckey a 'friend' that afternoon. Six days after Luckey was named a Meridian co-lead: SEQUENCE ONLY, no causal link shown.

FBI Vault Tesla file Part 01: his two-page report of Jan 30 1943 to Walter Gorsuch (Office of Alien Property), signed 'Technical Aide, Division 14, NDRC', judged Tesla's papers of no significant value.   Sources: <https://www.foxnews.com/politics/trump-floats-unexpected-name-successor-powerhouses-rubio-vance-stand> ; <https://x.com/cspan/status/2123853373191360990>
- **Kirkland & Ellis ↔ Donald Trump** — *settlement performance: Kirkland & Ellis (2025 $125M free-services deal with the Trump administration) reports at least $11.8M of free work for federal agencies (letter to lawmakers, Nov 2026)*, 2025-04-11 (deal, Truth Social) ; 2026-11-05 (letter, two days after the midterms) ; 2026-11-19 (Democrats' release) [V] · status: pending (2026-11-20) · added 2026-11-20 (v39) · last checked 2026-11-20  
  DONE (Bloomberg Law, Ryan Tarinelli, Nov 19 2026, updated Nov 20, opened, describing a letter from Kirkland's outside counsel Beth Wilkinson to lawmakers; House Judiciary Democrats release Nov 19 2026, opened): Kirkland & Ellis, which agreed in 2025 to $125M in free legal services after an EEOC inquiry (Trump's Apr 11 2025 Truth Social post), has provided at least $11.8M: about $4M to the Commerce Department, $2.2M to the Defense Department, $1.6M to the US Postal Service, $2M to the US International Development Finance Corporation and $1.8M representing an investment entity created under the US-Japan memorandum of understanding; more than 6,400 hours. The letter says the firm declined work on the Kennedy Center naming and on challenges to USPS ballot transmittal. SAID (Raskin, Blumenthal, Schiff): 'this corrupt bargain went much further than originally understood'; they sent follow-up letters Nov 19 to nine firms (Latham, Simpson Thacher, Willkie, Sullivan & Cromwell, Cadwalader, A&O Shearman, Milbank, Paul Weiss, Skadden). Kirkland's letter says the deal had 'little impact' on its pro bono work (Reuters via Al Jazeera). 'Coercion' is the lawmakers' word, not a finding.  
  Sources: <https://news.bloomberglaw.com/business-and-practice/kirkland-did-millions-in-work-for-trump-agencies-lawmakers-say> ; <https://democrats-judiciary.house.gov/media-center/press-releases/after-one-law-firm-reveals-being-coerced-into-providing-118-million-in-free-legal-work-to-trump-administration-ranking-members-raskin-and-blumenthal-and-sen-schiff-slam-nine-other-law-firms-for-stonewalling-congressional> ; <https://www.aljazeera.com/news/2026/11/20/democrats-say-law-firm-gave-11-8m-worth-of-service-to-trump-administration>

- **JD Vance ↔ Mohsen Rezaei** — *contact (letter to Iran's Supreme National Security Council secretary proposing renewed talks, via Qatari mediators) - DONE per US officials; Trump SAID it was about medical supplies*, 2026-10 (sent ~week of Oct 19, delivered Sun Oct 25 per NYT; reported Oct 28) [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-28 (v22) · last checked 2026-10-28

NYT (senior US official + Iranian official): letter sent last week via Qatari mediators, delivered Sunday, proposing immediate comprehensive talks incl. the nuclear program. Axios (US official): Vance suggested the letter; 'The President was enthusiastic'; talks 'require discussions on the nuclear issue'. Starlink has the option'; NATO Amb. Whitaker 'They are a private company'.   SAID/DONE (DOJ Office of Public Affairs, Tue Nov 17 2026, opened): Commerce and DOJ say Commerce opened an export-control investigation of Megaspeed in 2024; in May 2025 Commerce asked Nvidia for records of direct or indirect sales to Megaspeed; in June 2025 Nvidia put Megaspeed on its 'no sell' list; BIS agents with Singaporean officers did an on-site end-use check in June 2025 and interviewed CEO Alice Huang; DOJ then took the lead in the criminal investigation. The statement says it addresses 'inaccurate information circulating in public reporting' without saying which, credits 'the Department of Commerce under Secretary Howard Lutnick' and DOJ 'under Attorney General Todd Blanche', and charges no one. Timing: DOJ page dated Tue Nov 17; CNBC (published 1:36 AM EST Nov 17, opened) says it came out Monday evening US time and that Lutnick reposted it ('very proud of the work of the Bureau of Industry and Security'); CNBC also quotes CFR's Chris McGuire ('The facts here just don't add up') and Singapore's MHA (Alice Huang not investigated for espionage; barred from re-entry Feb 2026). It lands while Lutnick faces a Dec 10 Senate Banking hearing and Bessent's on-record account (Nov 13) that four agencies favored listing Megaspeed 'for months'. Sequence only; the statement does not mention any Entity List decision.

  SAID (named official, on camera; CNBC Squawk Box Nov 13 2026, quoted in CNBC's Luke Fountain article; CNBC = NBCUniversal/Comcast): Treasury Secretary Bessent said the departments of Treasury, Defense and State and USTR 'were in favor of putting Megaspeed on the entity list for months,' and that Commerce is responsible for the Entity List: 'You will have to ask them about their internal processes.' This is the first on-record version of the split the Nov 11 Axios account (anonymous; Commerce: 'This is false') described. Megaspeed is not on the Entity List as of build. The Squawk Box transcript/video itself was not opened.  
  Sources: <https://www.cnbc.com/2026/11/13/howard-lutnick-nvidia-jensen-huang-china-cantor-fitzgerald.html>
- **Scott Bessent ↔ Nvidia** — *public demand on a company board (Treasury Secretary on Fox News 'Sunday Morning Futures', Nov 15 2026)*, 2026-11-15 [V] · status: active · added 2026-11-16 (v35) · last checked 2026-11-16  
  SAID (Fox Business, Max Bacall, Nov 15 2026 2:25 PM EST, opened; Fox Corp): Bessent called for Nvidia's board to hire its next CEO from outside the company: 'They need a dramatic shake-up'; 'We will see if Nvidia intentionally ignored the warning signs of these shell companies, many of them in Singapore'; demanded answers 'in the next 10 days from the board and from the outside counsel'. CNBC (Nov 15/16, opened; NBCUniversal/Comcast) quotes: 'were they pressing these seemingly illicit sales to goose their revenues and their earnings?'. No Treasury action or document accompanies the statement; Treasury has no stated authority over Nvidia's board. The day after, Trump SAID he 'would not mind' Huang returning (separate edge): two named officials, opposite positions.  
  Sources: <https://www.foxbusiness.com/business-leaders/bessent-calls-nvidia-leadership-shake-up-amid-china-chip-scandal-urges-outside-ceo> ; <https://www.cnbc.com/2026/11/16/nvidia-jensen-huang-return-tech.html>

Rubio: 'the Microsoft incident has nothing to do with what we saw with the FAA. The FAA has nothing to do with what we saw with the banks'; 'We are not under systemic attack'. Same day (register row, prepared House Homeland Security testimony): DHS Secretary Mullin SAID Iranian government cyber actors 'have targeted U.S. critical infrastructure networks since the conflict with Iran began'; that testimony does not mention the outages. ## Pam Bondi
- **Pam Bondi ↔ Qatar (Embassy) via Ballard Partners** — *foreign agent (FARA) ; employment*, 2019-07 - 2020 [V] · status: active · added 2026-10-09 (v5) · last checked 2026-10-09

  Sources: <https://www.documentcloud.org/documents/25993306-doj-epstein-july-2025-memo/> ; <https://apnews.com/article/epstein-files-dan-bongino-bondi-trump-fbi-0f02f67b6c97fc50e4d93bc4dbf4c4d0>

## Nvidia

  SAID (I): The New York Times (Oct 29 2026; relayed by Tom's Hardware, fetched): US officials concluded Alice Huang (Huang Le), a Megaspeed executive, is a Chinese intelligence asset passing information to Beijing and that she has a close relationship with Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang (seen together around Computex 2024 and 2025); Trump was briefed about a year ago and took no action; Jensen Huang has not received a defensive briefing; officials do not believe he knows. Megaspeed (Nvidia cloud partner) is under a Commerce probe since late 2024 over possible chip diversion, handed to DOJ. ON RECORD: Nvidia: Jensen's interactions with Ms. Huang are 'not unusual or unexpected'; customers get 'ordinary business information' and 'zero information relevant to national security'; Chinese embassy: 'groundless smears'. Graded I: no US agency statement; anonymous officials. Why Trump did not act = TK (outlets relay reluctance to hurt Nvidia sales; that is reporting, not a statement).  
  Sources: <https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/policy/nvidia-ceo-jensen-huang-may-have-been-the-target-of-a-spy-plot-through-chinese-intelligence-asset-report-claims-exec-from-firm-under-investigation-for-smuggling-banned-ai-chips-to-china-accused-of-being-a-spy-was-also-feeding-information-to-beijing> ; <https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/us/politics/nvidia-china-jensen-huang-trump.html>

  SAID (NYT, Ana Swanson and Devlin Barrett, Nov 9-10 2026, via The Next Web; Bloomberg via Yahoo Finance): in August 2025, during a BIS probe of Singapore-based Nvidia customer Megaspeed over suspected chip smuggling to China, Nvidia general counsel Tim Teter emailed the agents and wrote that Under Secretary Jeff Kessler had given Nvidia permission to reach out. Agents declined a meeting and forwarded the emails to Kessler, who (per a Commerce official) told them to handle the case by the book. The Justice Department then asked Commerce to pause; Commerce complied; DOJ runs a criminal inquiry (people familiar). Commerce SAID it handled the case by the book; Nvidia (Rizzo) SAID it consults officials to answer questions; Megaspeed denies smuggling. Kessler and DOJ did not comment. Contact and pause are reported by people familiar; no document is public. The pause came at DOJ's request, not shown to be caused by the email (sequence only).

## CoreCivic (private prisons / ICE detention)
- **CoreCivic (private prisons / ICE detention) ↔ ICE** — *contract / asset sale (DHS bought the idle 1,600-bed Prairie Correctional Facility from CoreCivic for ~$495.6M, Aug 4 2026; CoreCivic manages it for ICE to Aug 2031) vs CoreCivic's $75M offer to Minnesota (May 2025)*, 2025-05-07 ($75M offer to Minnesota DOC) ; 2026-08-04 (sale to DHS + ICE management contract) ; 2026-10-29 (KARE 11 records) [V] · status: active (2026-11-09) · added 2026-10-30 (v24) · last checked 2026-11-09  
  DONE (primary, CoreCivic 8-K, event Aug 4 2026): CoreCivic sold its 1,600-bed Prairie Correctional Facility (Appleton, Minnesota; idle since 2010) to the United States 'by and through the Department of Homeland Security' for approximately $495.6 million, and the 1,033-bed Midwest Regional Reception Center (Leavenworth, KS) for ~$238.4 million, both closing on signing; CoreCivic 'recently entered into a management agreement with ICE' for Prairie, expiring August 2031. DONE (records obtained by KARE 11, Oct 29 2026): a May 7 2025 email from CoreCivic to Minnesota's Deputy Corrections Commissioner offered the same facility to the state for $75M 'inclusive of the upgrades'; Minnesota declined. The gap is about $420M. SAID: CoreCivic (written statement): independent appraisers used a cost-based methodology and 'the federal government ultimately determined the sale price'; Minnesota Corrections Commissioner Paul Schnell: 'unexplainable'; Rep. Betty McCollum calls for a congressional investigation, citing the owners as 'mega donors'; DHS Secretary Markwayne Mullin's office did not answer KARE 11. GAO (Sep 2026, per KARE 11) warned ICE facility purchases risk waste. NOT established: how DHS set the price, any appraisal record, or any link between CoreCivic's donations and the price (time order only; see corecivic-trump-inaugural-500k-2024).

## Steve Witkoff / Jared Kushner

Topics: team-level Ukraine-US-Russia trilateral in less than a week (Abu Dhabi Nov 10-11 per press), Patriot interceptors, Drone Deal bilateral track, ironclad postwar security guarantees; same day Brussels recovery meeting (US/EU/France/UK/Germany/NATO/World Bank/business). Call content is Zelenskyy's readout (BOTH); Witkoff/Kushner had not publicly commented at report time. No causation claimed beyond the documented contact.

SAID (Zelensky on X, Nov 18, register rows): the envoys will debrief in Berlin on the talks in China. Scheduled, not held at build. Axios: Ushakov said no agreement had been reached that Europe would have a seat at the table.

- **Steve Witkoff / Jared Kushner ↔ Friedrich Merz (German Chancellor)** — *US envoys attend the Coalition of the Willing summit hosted by Merz in Berlin (Nov 19 2026), hours after the Shenzhen trilateral; Trump calls in from Air Force One*, 2026-11-19 [V] · status: active (2026-11-19) · added 2026-11-19 (v38) · last checked 2026-11-19  
  DONE (AFP, Nov 19 2026 21:03, opened; NATO news release Nov 19 and German Chancellery press-conference transcript, search pages seen; Ukrinform Nov 19 15:18, search page seen): Witkoff and Kushner attended the Berlin Coalition of the Willing summit hosted by Chancellor Friedrich Merz, co-chaired by Macron and UK PM Andy Burnham, with Zelensky, more than 25 leaders (Chancellery: almost 40 delegations) and the EU and NATO chiefs; Merz thanked both envoys by name at the joint press conference. SAID (Kushner, per AFP): Trump called into the talks from Air Force One; 'President Trump wants to make a deal with Europe by his side. He's not looking to impose anything unilaterally'; an 'Article Five-like security guarantee' is 'something that President Trump is open to, but obviously it has to be in America's interest'. SAID (Merz, AFP): new talks 'soon' with Europe at the table alongside the US, Russia and Ukraine; a neutral venue still sought, the UAE named as possible; 'a Yalta format cannot bring peace'; he called the China summit a 'photo op'. SAID (Zelensky on X, register row): a decision that Europeans join the negotiation formats; next meeting four-party (Ukraine, Europe, America, Russia). SAID (Witkoff, SEPeaceMissions, register row): talks 'frank and productive'. Held one day after it was scheduled; no date or venue set for a four-party round.

- **Russian intelligence services ↔ Loyal to Familia (LTF, banned Danish gang)** — *assessed tasking: Denmark's PET assesses Russian intelligence planned and initiated a thwarted Sept 25 2026 assassination attempt near Copenhagen, to be carried out by people linked to the banned gang LTF*, 2026-09-25 (thwarted) ; 2026-11-20 (PET 16:39 Copenhagen = 10:39 AM EST) [V] · status: pending (2026-11-20) · added 2026-11-20 (v39) · last checked 2026-11-20

## Locator (v37 grid)

v37 locator: every overview and cluster panel PNG has a printed grid around its graph area (column letters along the top and bottom, row numbers down both sides, faint dotted cell lines). For each file, nodes[name] = pixel box [x0,y0,x1,y1] in that PNG and its grid cell; edges['<edge number>'] = the pixel point of the '#n' label and its cell. Coordinates come from the same graphviz layout pass that drew the image (dot -Tjson0, scaled to the PNG). Use: find an actor by cell on the picture, or crop bbox to read one node. Positions are where a box is drawn, not a measure of importance.

powermap.png: [25869, 39645] px, grid A-T x 1-13, cell 1320 px, origin [187, 2240]; 362/364 node boxes have a drawn border pixel at the left edge (+/-3 px).

- [N] node **Loyal to Familia (LTF, banned Danish gang)** (page p05): Criminal organisation banned by Denmark's Supreme Court (2021); people linked to it charged (Nov 2026) with attempted murder and assisting a foreign intelligence service

## Machine link (v36 QR stamp)

Every overview, cluster panel and the summary card carry a QR code with the same line printed beside it, so a screenshot or re-upload (which strips PNG text chunks) still leads back to the exact data release. Payload format: `POWERMAP <version> asof=<YYYY-MM-DD> n=<len(nodes)> e=<len(edges)> img=<png file name> data=<public powermap.json URL>`.

- `powermap-defense-intel.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-defense-intel.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- `powermap-epstein-2009-2017.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-epstein-2009-2017.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- `powermap-government.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-government.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- `powermap-gulf-sovereign.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-gulf-sovereign.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- `powermap-iran-yemen.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-iran-yemen.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- `powermap-media-owners.png`: `POWERMAP v39 asof=2026-11-20 n=364 e=488 img=powermap-media-owners.png data=https://signaloftheeye.github.io/civcurve/powermap/powermap.json` (decode check: ok)
- ... 13 files in all (powermap.json -> qr_stamp)
